@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import type { MarkstreamRendererHandle, MarkstreamVirtualMetrics } from 'markstream-vue'
+import type { ChatMessage } from '@/store/chat/types'
+
+interface Registration { source: string, final: boolean, renderer: MarkstreamRendererHandle, notify(): void, ready: boolean, settling: boolean }
+const renderers = new WeakMap<Element, Registration>()
+const complete = (metrics: MarkstreamVirtualMetrics) => metrics.final && metrics.stable && metrics.renderedCount >= metrics.nodeCount
+
+/** markstream 2.0 emits render-final only with virtualScroll enabled. Chat keeps
+ * its existing nonvirtual renderer and uses the public metrics/settle handle.
+ * No own quiet timer, length guess or virtual-scroll policy is introduced. */
+export function bindSpeechRenderer(body: Element, renderer: MarkstreamRendererHandle, source: string, final: boolean, notify: () => void) {
+  const previous = renderers.get(body)
+  if (previous?.renderer === renderer && previous.source === source && previous.final === final) return
+  renderers.set(body, { source, final, renderer, notify, ready: false, settling: false })
+}
+function ready(body: Element, source: string) {
+  const registered = renderers.get(body)
+  if (!registered || registered.source !== source || !registered.final || !body.isConnected) return false
+  if (registered.ready) return true
+  if (complete(registered.renderer.getVirtualMetrics())) { registered.ready = true; registered.notify(); return true }
+  if (!registered.settling) {
+    registered.settling = true
+    // Reuse renderer-owned settlement and cancellation/version checks. Its
+    // defaults are not a product latency promise; unstable is never complete.
+    void registered.renderer.settle({ flushPendingTimers: true }).then(metrics => {
+      registered.settling = false
+      if (renderers.get(body) !== registered || !body.isConnected || !complete(metrics)) return
+      registered.ready = true; registered.notify()
+    }).catch(() => { registered.settling = false })
+  }
+  return false
+}
+export function speechRenderComplete(message: ChatMessage, element: Element | null) {
+  if (message.role !== 'assistant' || !element?.isConnected) return false
+  const bodies = Array.from(element.querySelectorAll('[data-speech-body]'))
+  return message.messages.filter(block => block.type === 'text' && block.content).every(block => {
+    const body = bodies.find(body => body.getAttribute('data-speech-block-id') === String(block.id))
+    return !!body && ready(body, block.type === 'text' ? block.content : '')
+  })
+}

+// Package cosyvoiceinput adds the documented CosyVoice duplex input contract to
+// the existing explicitly registered Twilight CosyVoice provider. It is not a
+// generic Qwen/model-name dispatcher. Fixed-text methods remain Twilight-owned.
+package cosyvoiceinput
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"net"
+	"net/http"
+	"net/url"
+	"strconv"
+	"strings"
+	"sync"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	speech "github.com/felinics/twilight/provider/alibabacloud/speech"
+	sdk "github.com/felinics/twilight/sdk"
+	"github.com/google/uuid"
+	"github.com/gorilla/websocket"
+
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/audio/input"
+)
+
+var (
+	ErrSequence = errors.New("speech input sequence or offset mismatch")
+	ErrFinished = errors.New("speech input is closed")
+	ErrProtocol = errors.New("invalid CosyVoice input session response")
+)
+
+// Provider preserves all existing SDK methods and opts in to input.Provider.
+type Provider struct {
+	*speech.Provider
+	endpoint, key string
+}
+
+func New(key, endpoint string) *Provider {
+	opts := []speech.Option{speech.WithAPIKey(key)}
+	if endpoint != "" {
+		opts = append(opts, speech.WithBaseURL(endpoint))
+	} else {
+		endpoint = "wss://dashscope.aliyuncs.com/api-ws/v1/inference/"
+	}
+	return &Provider{Provider: speech.New(opts...), endpoint: endpoint, key: key}
+}
+
+// InputContentType validates the exact protocol/configuration without dialing.
+func (p *Provider) InputContentType(params sdk.SpeechParams) (string, error) {
+	// Remote TLS remains verified by the normal dialer. Plain WS is confined to
+	// the loopback protocol fixture, never a production TLS override.
+	endpoint, err := url.Parse(p.endpoint)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", errors.New("invalid secure CosyVoice endpoint")
+	}
+	loopback := endpoint.Hostname() == "localhost" || net.ParseIP(endpoint.Hostname()).IsLoopback()
+	secure := endpoint.Scheme == "wss" || endpoint.Scheme == "ws" && loopback
+	if endpoint.User != nil || endpoint.RawQuery != "" || endpoint.Fragment != "" || !secure {
+		return "", errors.New("invalid secure CosyVoice endpoint")
+	}
+
+	if params.Model == nil || strings.TrimSpace(params.Model.ID) == "" {
+		return "", errors.New("speech input model is required")
+	}
+	voice, _ := params.Config["voice"].(string)
+	if strings.TrimSpace(voice) == "" {
+		return "", errors.New("speech input voice is required")
+	}
+	if ssml, _ := params.Config["enable_ssml"].(bool); ssml {
+		return "", errors.New("CosyVoice SSML does not support incremental input")
+	}
+	format, _ := params.Config["format"].(string)
+	if format == "" {
+		format = "mp3"
+	}
+	mime := map[string]string{"mp3": "audio/mpeg", "wav": "audio/wav", "pcm": "audio/pcm", "opus": "audio/opus"}[format]
+	if mime == "" {
+		return "", errors.New("invalid CosyVoice audio format")
+	}
+	return mime, nil
+}
+
+func (p *Provider) OpenInput(ctx context.Context, params sdk.SpeechParams) (input.Session, error) {
+	mime, err := p.InputContentType(params)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	voice, _ := params.Config["voice"].(string)
+	format, _ := params.Config["format"].(string)
+	if format == "" {
+		format = "mp3"
+	}
+
+	headers := http.Header{"Authorization": []string{"Bearer " + p.key}, "X-DashScope-DataInspection": []string{"enable"}}
+	conn, response, err := websocket.DefaultDialer.DialContext(ctx, p.endpoint, headers)
+	if response != nil && response.Body != nil {
+		defer func() { _ = response.Body.Close() }()
+	}
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect CosyVoice input: %w", err)
+	}
+	sessionCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
+	s := &session{ctx: sessionCtx, cancel: cancel, conn: conn, task: uuid.NewString(), started: make(chan error, 1), done: make(chan struct{}), data: make(chan []byte, 8), errors: make(chan error, 1)}
+	s.audio = sdk.NewSpeechStreamResult(s.data, mime, s.errors)
+	parameters := map[string]any{"text_type": "PlainText", "voice": voice, "format": format, "sample_rate": 22050}
+	// Match the existing configured Twilight fields. No model/voice defaults,
+	// provider switch, secret config passthrough or invented context overlap.
+	// Preserve pinned Twilight numeric parsing and omission of zero controls;
+	// protocol defaults remain unchanged. Model and voice are never substituted.
+	for _, key := range []string{"sample_rate", "volume", "rate", "pitch"} {
+		if value, ok := params.Config[key]; ok {
+			number, err := strconv.ParseFloat(fmt.Sprint(value), 64)
+			if err != nil {
+				continue
+			}
+			if key == "sample_rate" {
+				parameters[key] = int(number)
+			} else if number != 0 {
+				parameters[key] = number
+			}
+		}
+	}
+
+	command := s.command("run-task", map[string]any{})
+	command["payload"] = map[string]any{"task_group": "audio", "task": "tts", "function": "SpeechSynthesizer", "model": params.Model.ID, "parameters": parameters, "input": map[string]any{}}
+	stop := context.AfterFunc(sessionCtx, func() { _ = conn.Close() })
+	if err = conn.WriteJSON(command); err != nil {
+		stop()
+		s.Cancel()
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	go s.read(stop)
+	select {
+	case err = <-s.started:
+		if err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		return s, nil
+	case <-sessionCtx.Done():
+		return nil, sessionCtx.Err()
+	}
+}
+
+type session struct {
+	ctx              context.Context
+	cancel           context.CancelFunc
+	conn             *websocket.Conn
+	task             string
+	mu               sync.Mutex
+	sequence, offset uint64
+	final            bool
+	started          chan error
+	done             chan struct{}
+	data             chan []byte
+	errors           chan error
+	audio            *sdk.SpeechStreamResult
+}
+
+func (s *session) Audio() *sdk.SpeechStreamResult { return s.audio }
+func (s *session) Cancel()                        { s.cancel(); _ = s.conn.Close() }
+func (s *session) command(action string, payload map[string]any) map[string]any {
+	return map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"action": action, "task_id": s.task, "streaming": "duplex"}, "payload": map[string]any{"input": payload}}
+}
+
+func (s *session) Append(ctx context.Context, frame input.Frame) error {
+	s.mu.Lock()
+	defer s.mu.Unlock()
+	if s.ctx.Err() != nil {
+		return s.ctx.Err()
+	}
+	if ctx.Err() != nil {
+		return ctx.Err()
+	}
+	if s.final {
+		return ErrFinished
+	}
+	if frame.Sequence != s.sequence || frame.Offset != s.offset {
+		return ErrSequence
+	}
+	if !utf8.ValidString(frame.Text) || (frame.Text == "" && !frame.Final) {
+		return errors.New("invalid empty or UTF-8 speech input")
+	}
+	var count uint64
+	for range frame.Text {
+		count++
+		if count > 20000 {
+			return errors.New("CosyVoice input limit exceeded")
+		}
+	}
+	if s.offset+count > 200000 {
+		return errors.New("CosyVoice input limit exceeded")
+	}
+	// A canceled/uncertain write closes this whole task; never replay an append.
+	stop := context.AfterFunc(ctx, func() { s.Cancel() })
+	defer stop()
+	if frame.Text != "" {
+		if err := s.conn.WriteJSON(s.command("continue-task", map[string]any{"text": frame.Text})); err != nil {
+			s.Cancel()
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	if frame.Final {
+		s.final = true
+		if err := s.conn.WriteJSON(s.command("finish-task", map[string]any{})); err != nil {
+			s.Cancel()
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	s.sequence++
+	s.offset += count
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (s *session) read(stop func() bool) {
+	var result error
+	started := false
+	defer func() {
+		if s.ctx.Err() != nil {
+			result = s.ctx.Err()
+		}
+		if !started {
+			if result == nil {
+				result = ErrProtocol
+			}
+			s.started <- result
+		}
+		stop()
+		s.cancel()
+		_ = s.conn.Close()
+		if result != nil {
+			s.errors <- result
+		}
+		close(s.errors)
+		close(s.data)
+		close(s.done)
+	}()
+	for {
+		kind, bytes, err := s.conn.ReadMessage()
+		if err != nil {
+			result = err
+			return
+		}
+		if kind == websocket.BinaryMessage {
+			if !started {
+				result = ErrProtocol
+				return
+			}
+			if len(bytes) > 0 {
+				select {
+				case s.data <- bytes:
+				case <-s.ctx.Done():
+					return
+				}
+			}
+			continue
+		}
+		if kind != websocket.TextMessage {
+			result = ErrProtocol
+			return
+		}
+		var event struct {
+			Header struct {
+				Event  string `json:"event"`
+				TaskID string `json:"task_id"`
+			} `json:"header"`
+		}
+		if err = json.Unmarshal(bytes, &event); err != nil || event.Header.TaskID != s.task {
+			result = ErrProtocol
+			return
+		}
+		switch event.Header.Event {
+		case "task-started":
+			if started {
+				result = ErrProtocol
+				return
+			}
+			started = true
+			s.started <- nil
+		case "result-generated": // Provider-owned metadata is not text or extra audio.
+		case "task-finished":
+			s.mu.Lock()
+			finished := s.final
+			s.mu.Unlock()
+			if !started || !finished {
+				result = ErrProtocol
+			}
+			return
+		case "task-failed":
+			result = errors.New("CosyVoice input task failed")
+			return
+		default:
+			result = ErrProtocol
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}

+package cosyvoiceinput
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"errors"
+	"net/http"
+	"net/http/httptest"
+	"strings"
+	"sync"
+	"testing"
+	"time"
+
+	speech "github.com/felinics/twilight/provider/alibabacloud/speech"
+	sdk "github.com/felinics/twilight/sdk"
+	"github.com/gorilla/websocket"
+
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/audio/input"

+type fixture struct {
+	mu       sync.Mutex
+	commands []map[string]any
+	server   *httptest.Server
+}
+
+func newFixture(t *testing.T) *fixture {
+	t.Helper()
+	f := &fixture{}
+	f.server = httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
+		c, err := (&websocket.Upgrader{}).Upgrade(w, r, nil)
+		if err != nil {
+			return
+		}
+		defer func() { _ = c.Close() }()
+		for {
+			var command map[string]any
+			if c.ReadJSON(&command) != nil {
+				return
+			}
+			f.mu.Lock()
+			f.commands = append(f.commands, command)
+			f.mu.Unlock()
+			header := command["header"].(map[string]any)
+			id := header["task_id"]
+			action := header["action"]
+			switch action {
+			case "run-task":
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-started", "task_id": id}})
+			case "continue-task":
+				_ = c.WriteMessage(websocket.BinaryMessage, []byte{1, 2})
+			case "finish-task":
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-finished", "task_id": id}})
+				return
+			}
+		}
+	}))
+	t.Cleanup(f.server.Close)
+	return f
+}
+func (f *fixture) url() string { return "ws" + strings.TrimPrefix(f.server.URL, "http") }
+func (f *fixture) actions() []string {
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+	var out []string
+	for _, c := range f.commands {
+		out = append(out, c["header"].(map[string]any)["action"].(string))
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func TestPinnedFixedTextCannotKeepTwoInputsInOneTask(t *testing.T) {
+	f := newFixture(t)
+	p := speech.New(speech.WithBaseURL(f.url()), speech.WithAPIKey("synthetic-only"))
+	for _, text := range []string{"甲😀", "乙。"} {
+		s, err := p.DoStream(context.Background(), sdk.SpeechParams{Model: &sdk.SpeechModel{ID: "configured-fixture-model"}, Text: text, Config: map[string]any{"voice": "configured-fixture-voice"}})
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if _, err = s.Bytes(); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	count := 0
+	for _, a := range f.actions() {
+		if a == "run-task" {
+			count++
+		}
+	}
+	if count != 2 {
+		b, _ := json.Marshal(f.actions())
+		t.Fatalf("negative control: pinned fixed-text calls should expose two tasks, got %d, commands=%s", count, b)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestNativeInputKeepsContextAcrossUnicodeFramesAndFinishesOnlyAfterTail(t *testing.T) {
+	f := newFixture(t)
+	p := New("synthetic-only", f.url())
+	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second*3)
+	defer cancel()
+	s, err := p.OpenInput(ctx, sdk.SpeechParams{Model: &sdk.SpeechModel{ID: "configured-fixture-model"}, Config: map[string]any{"voice": "configured-fixture-voice", "format": "mp3", "sample_rate": 24000}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer s.Cancel()
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 0, Offset: 0, Text: "甲😀"}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	select {
+	case audio := <-s.Audio().Stream:
+		if string(audio) != string([]byte{1, 2}) {
+			t.Fatalf("audio %v", audio)
+		}
+	case <-ctx.Done():
+		t.Fatal("no audio before input finish")
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 0, Offset: 0, Text: "duplicate"}); !errors.Is(err, ErrSequence) {
+		t.Fatalf("duplicate %v", err)
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 1, Offset: 1, Text: "wrong offset"}); !errors.Is(err, ErrSequence) {
+		t.Fatalf("offset %v", err)
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 1, Offset: 2, Text: "乙。"}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 2, Offset: 4, Final: true}); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if _, err = s.Audio().Bytes(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	want := []string{"run-task", "continue-task", "continue-task", "finish-task"}
+	got := f.actions()
+	if strings.Join(got, ",") != strings.Join(want, ",") {
+		t.Fatalf("commands %v", got)
+	}
+	f.mu.Lock()
+	defer f.mu.Unlock()
+	id := f.commands[0]["header"].(map[string]any)["task_id"]
+	for _, command := range f.commands {
+		if command["header"].(map[string]any)["task_id"] != id {
+			t.Fatal("context restarted")
+		}
+	}
+	payload := f.commands[0]["payload"].(map[string]any)
+	if payload["model"] != "configured-fixture-model" || payload["parameters"].(map[string]any)["voice"] != "configured-fixture-voice" {
+		t.Fatal("configuration changed")
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Sequence: 3, Offset: 4, Text: "late"}); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("accepted after finish")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestCancelClosesBlockedReadAndNeverAcceptsLateInput(t *testing.T) {
+	f := newFixture(t)
+	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
+	s, err := New("synthetic-only", f.url()).OpenInput(ctx, sdk.SpeechParams{Model: &sdk.SpeechModel{ID: "configured-fixture-model"}, Config: map[string]any{"voice": "configured-fixture-voice"}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	cancel()
+	done := make(chan error, 1)
+	go func() { _, err := s.Audio().Bytes(); done <- err }()
+	select {
+	case err := <-done:
+		if !errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
+			t.Fatalf("cancel %v", err)
+		}
+	case <-time.After(time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("blocked read did not cancel")
+	}
+	if err = s.Append(context.Background(), input.Frame{Text: "late"}); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("accepted canceled input")
+	}
+	if got := f.actions(); len(got) != 1 || got[0] != "run-task" {
+		t.Fatalf("post-cancel commands %v", got)
+	}
+}

+func TestInputRejectsEarlyEOFOrForeignTaskInsteadOfCachingCompleteAudio(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, event := range []string{"task-finished", "foreign"} {
+		t.Run(event, func(t *testing.T) {
+			server := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
+				c, err := (&websocket.Upgrader{}).Upgrade(w, r, nil)
+				if err != nil {
+					return
+				}
+				defer func() { _ = c.Close() }()
+				var command map[string]any
+				if c.ReadJSON(&command) != nil {
+					return
+				}
+				id := command["header"].(map[string]any)["task_id"]
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-started", "task_id": id}})
+				if c.ReadJSON(&command) != nil {
+					return
+				}
+				_ = c.WriteMessage(websocket.BinaryMessage, []byte{1, 2})
+				if event == "foreign" {
+					id = "another-task"
+				}
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-finished", "task_id": id}})
+			}))
+			defer server.Close()
+			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), time.Second*3)
+			defer cancel()
+			s, err := New("synthetic-only", "ws"+strings.TrimPrefix(server.URL, "http")).OpenInput(ctx, sdk.SpeechParams{Model: &sdk.SpeechModel{ID: "configured-fixture-model"}, Config: map[string]any{"voice": "configured-fixture-voice"}})
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			defer s.Cancel()
+			if err = s.Append(ctx, input.Frame{Text: "not finished"}); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			bytes, err := s.Audio().Bytes()
+			if string(bytes) != string([]byte{1, 2}) || !errors.Is(err, ErrProtocol) {
+				t.Fatalf("bytes=%v error=%v", bytes, err)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+// Package input describes optional same-session text ingress without changing
+// Twilight's fixed-text SpeechProvider interface. Providers opt in explicitly.
+package input
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"errors"
+
+	sdk "github.com/felinics/twilight/sdk"
+)
+
+var ErrUnsupported = errors.New("configured speech protocol does not support incremental input")
+
+// Frame offsets count Unicode code points in the exact spoken-text projection.
+// Sequence and Offset both start at zero. Final closes input, not audio output.
+// Unknown or rejected writes must never be retried as another synthesis.
+type Frame struct {
+	Sequence uint64 `json:"sequence"`
+	Offset   uint64 `json:"offset"`
+	Text     string `json:"text"`
+	Final    bool   `json:"final"`
+}
+
+type Session interface {
+	Append(context.Context, Frame) error
+	Audio() *sdk.SpeechStreamResult
+	Cancel()
+}
+
+type Provider interface {
+	OpenInput(context.Context, sdk.SpeechParams) (Session, error)
+	InputContentType(sdk.SpeechParams) (string, error)
+}

+func TestPlaybackInputCapabilityIsExplicitProtocolReadOnlyAndHonorsDisabledOrSSML(t *testing.T) {
+	var dials atomic.Int32
+	previous := websocket.DefaultDialer
+	copyDialer := *previous
+	copyDialer.NetDialContext = func(context.Context, string, string) (net.Conn, error) {
+		dials.Add(1)
+		return nil, errors.New("unexpected dial")
+	}
+	websocket.DefaultDialer = &copyDialer
+	t.Cleanup(func() { websocket.DefaultDialer = previous })
+	id, _ := db.ParseUUID("10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002")
+	providerID, _ := db.ParseUUID("10000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001")
+	q := &playbackStreamQueries{provider: sqlc.Provider{ID: providerID, ClientType: string(models.ClientTypeAlibabaSpeech), Enable: true, Config: []byte(`{"api_key":"synthetic-only","base_url":"wss://fixture.invalid/api-ws/v1/inference/"}`)}, model: sqlc.GetSpeechModelWithProviderRow{ID: id, ProviderID: providerID, ModelID: "configured-model-no-name-heuristic", Enable: true, Config: []byte(`{"voice":"configured-fixture-voice","format":"mp3"}`)}}
+	service := NewService(slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), q, NewRegistry())
+	identity, mime, err := service.PlaybackInputInfo(context.Background(), id.String())
+	if err != nil || mime != "audio/mpeg" || len(identity) != 64 {
+		t.Fatalf("info mime=%s err=%v", mime, err)
+	}
+	old, err := service.PlaybackIdentity(context.Background(), id.String())
+	if err != nil || old != identity {
+		t.Fatal("input identity diverged from effective existing config")
+	}
+	q.model.Config = []byte(`{"voice":"configured-fixture-voice","format":"mp3","enable_ssml":true}`)
+	if _, mime, err = service.PlaybackInputInfo(context.Background(), id.String()); err != nil || mime != "" {
+		t.Fatal("SSML advertised incremental input")
+	}
+	q.model.Config = []byte(`{"voice":"configured-fixture-voice","format":"mp3"}`)
+	q.provider.Enable = false
+	if _, mime, err = service.PlaybackInputInfo(context.Background(), id.String()); err != nil || mime != "" {
+		t.Fatal("disabled provider advertised input")
+	}
+	if _, _, err = service.OpenPlaybackInput(context.Background(), id.String()); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("disabled provider opened input")
+	}
+	q.provider.Enable = true
+	q.provider.ClientType = string(models.ClientTypeOpenAISpeech)
+	q.model.ModelID = "cosyvoice-looking-model-name"
+	if _, mime, err = service.PlaybackInputInfo(context.Background(), id.String()); err != nil || mime != "" {
+		t.Fatal("model name guessed native protocol")
+	}
+	if dials.Load() != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("metadata caused %d network calls", dials.Load())
+	}
+}

+// PlaybackInputInfo only reads the configured provider's explicit input capability.
+// Empty content type means fixed-text only; names/model strings never opt in.
+func (s *Service) PlaybackInputInfo(ctx context.Context, modelID string) (identity, contentType string, err error) {
+	params, err := s.resolveSpeechParams(ctx, modelID, "", nil)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", "", err
+	}
+	if !params.providerEnabled {
+		return params.identity, "", nil
+	}
+	provider, ok := params.model.Provider.(input.Provider)
+	if !ok {
+		return params.identity, "", nil
+	}
+	contentType, err = provider.InputContentType(sdk.SpeechParams{Model: params.model, Config: params.config})
+	if err != nil {
+		return params.identity, "", nil
+	}
+	return params.identity, contentType, nil
+}
+
+func (s *Service) OpenPlaybackInput(ctx context.Context, modelID string) (input.Session, string, error) {
+	params, err := s.resolveSpeechParams(ctx, modelID, "", nil)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, "", err
+	}
+	if !params.providerEnabled {
+		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: provider disabled", input.ErrUnsupported)
+	}
+	provider, ok := params.model.Provider.(input.Provider)
+	if !ok {
+		return nil, "", input.ErrUnsupported
+	}
+	if _, err = provider.InputContentType(sdk.SpeechParams{Model: params.model, Config: params.config}); err != nil {
+		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("%w: %w", input.ErrUnsupported, err)
+	}
+	session, err := provider.OpenInput(ctx, sdk.SpeechParams{Model: params.model, Config: params.config})
+	return session, params.identity, err
+}

 type synthesizeRequest struct {
diff --git a/internal/handlers/bot_tts_input.go b/internal/handlers/bot_tts_input.go
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..355d3557d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/internal/handlers/bot_tts_input.go

+package handlers
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"errors"
+	"net/http"
+	"strings"
+	"sync"
+	"unicode/utf8"
+
+	"github.com/google/uuid"
+	"github.com/labstack/echo/v4"
+
+	"github.com/felinics/memoh/internal/audio/input"
+)
+
+type playbackInputService interface {
+	PlaybackInputInfo(context.Context, string) (string, string, error)
+	OpenPlaybackInput(context.Context, string) (input.Session, string, error)
+}
+
+type playbackInputRequest struct {
+	ID string `json:"id"`
+	input.Frame
+}
+
+type playbackInputSessions struct {
+	sync.Mutex
+	entries map[string]*playbackInputSession
+}
+type playbackInputSession struct {
+	session           input.Session
+	identity, modelID string
+	cancel            context.CancelFunc
+}
+
+func inputRequest(c echo.Context) (playbackInputRequest, error) {
+	var request playbackInputRequest
+	if err := c.Bind(&request); err != nil {
+		return request, err
+	}
+	id, err := uuid.Parse(request.ID)
+	if err != nil || id == uuid.Nil {
+		return request, echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid playback input ID")
+	}
+	request.ID = id.String()
+	if !utf8.ValidString(request.Text) || utf8.RuneCountInString(request.Text) > 20000 || (strings.TrimSpace(request.Text) == "" && !request.Final) {
+		return request, echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid playback input text")
+	}
+	return request, nil
+}
+
+func inputOwner(c echo.Context, botID, id string) (string, error) {
+	actor, err := RequireChannelIdentityID(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	return actor + ":" + botID + ":" + id, nil
+}
+
+// StartPlaybackInput godoc
+// @Summary Start one configured native incremental speech task
+// @Description Requires an active account JWT and Bot chat permission. Streams one task's audio until final input and provider completion. Maximum 20000 Unicode characters per frame, within the native protocol limit. Never retry an uncertain synthesis. No message or attachment is created.
+// @Tags bots
+// @Accept json
+// @Produce octet-stream
+// @Param bot_id path string true "Bot ID"
+// @Param request body playbackInputRequest true "Initial exact spoken-text frame, sequence and Unicode offset zero"
+// @Success 200 {file} file "Configured audio bytes"
+// @Failure 400 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 401 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 403 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 409 {object} server.Problem
+// @Router /bots/{bot_id}/tts/playback/input [post].
+func (h *BotAudioHandler) StartPlaybackInput(c echo.Context) error {
+	botID, err := h.authorizePlayback(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	request, err := inputRequest(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if strings.TrimSpace(request.Text) == "" {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusBadRequest, "initial speech text is required")
+	}
+	if request.Sequence != 0 || request.Offset != 0 {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "playback input must start at zero")
+	}
+	service, ok := h.audioService.(playbackInputService)
+	if !ok {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "native input is unavailable")
+	}
+	settings, err := h.settingsService.GetBot(c.Request().Context(), botID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if settings.TtsModelID == "" {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "speech model is not configured")
+	}
+	key, err := inputOwner(c, botID, request.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(c.Request().Context())
+	defer cancel()
+	active := &playbackInputSession{modelID: settings.TtsModelID, cancel: cancel}
+	h.inputSessions.Lock()
+	if h.inputSessions.entries == nil {
+		h.inputSessions.entries = make(map[string]*playbackInputSession)
+	}
+	if h.inputSessions.entries[key] != nil {
+		h.inputSessions.Unlock()
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "playback input is already active")
+	}
+	h.inputSessions.entries[key] = active
+	h.inputSessions.Unlock()
+	defer func() { h.inputSessions.Lock(); delete(h.inputSessions.entries, key); h.inputSessions.Unlock() }()
+	session, identity, err := service.OpenPlaybackInput(ctx, settings.TtsModelID)
+	if errors.Is(err, input.ErrUnsupported) {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "native input is unavailable")
+	}
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	defer session.Cancel()
+	if err = session.Append(ctx, request.Frame); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	h.inputSessions.Lock()
+	active.session = session
+	active.identity = identity
+	h.inputSessions.Unlock()
+	stream := session.Audio()
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(stream.ContentType, "audio/") {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusBadGateway, "invalid playback content type")
+	}
+	c.Response().Header().Set("X-Speech-Playback-Identity", identity)
+	c.Response().Header().Set(echo.HeaderContentType, stream.ContentType)
+	c.Response().Header().Set(echo.HeaderCacheControl, "private, no-store")
+	c.Response().Header().Set(echo.HeaderXContentTypeOptions, "nosniff")
+	c.Response().Header().Set("X-Accel-Buffering", "no")
+	// Publish readiness before audio: a native model may need further text before
+	// producing its first frame. Waiting for audio here deadlocks duplex ingress.
+	c.Response().WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
+	c.Response().Flush()
+	for {
+		select {
+		case <-ctx.Done():
+			// An internal config cancellation must not become clean HTTP EOF:
+			// clients may otherwise cache truncated audio as a full reply.
+			panic(http.ErrAbortHandler)
+		case bytes, open := <-stream.Stream:
+			if !open {
+				if stream.Err() != nil {
+					panic(http.ErrAbortHandler)
+				}
+				return nil
+			}
+			if len(bytes) == 0 {
+				continue
+			}
+			if _, err = c.Response().Write(bytes); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			c.Response().Flush()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+// AppendPlaybackInput godoc
+// @Summary Append or finish the current native speech input
+// @Description Revalidates the active account, Bot chat permission and current effective configuration. Exact next sequence and Unicode offset are required. Missing/closed input never creates another synthesis.
+// @Tags bots
+// @Accept json
+// @Param bot_id path string true "Bot ID"
+// @Param request body playbackInputRequest true "Next frame; final closes text input, not audio output"
+// @Success 204
+// @Failure 400 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 401 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 403 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 404 {object} server.Problem
+// @Failure 409 {object} server.Problem
+// @Router /bots/{bot_id}/tts/playback/input [put].
+func (h *BotAudioHandler) AppendPlaybackInput(c echo.Context) error {
+	botID, err := h.authorizePlayback(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	request, err := inputRequest(c)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	key, err := inputOwner(c, botID, request.ID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	h.inputSessions.Lock()
+	active := h.inputSessions.entries[key]
+	var session input.Session
+	var identity, modelID string
+	var cancel context.CancelFunc
+	if active != nil {
+		session, identity, modelID, cancel = active.session, active.identity, active.modelID, active.cancel
+	}
+	h.inputSessions.Unlock()
+	if active == nil {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusNotFound, "playback input is unavailable")
+	}
+	if session == nil {
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "playback input is not ready")
+	}
+	settings, err := h.settingsService.GetBot(c.Request().Context(), botID)
+	if err != nil {
+		cancel()
+		return err
+	}
+	service, ok := h.audioService.(playbackInputService)
+	if !ok {
+		cancel()
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict)
+	}
+	current, _, err := service.PlaybackInputInfo(c.Request().Context(), settings.TtsModelID)
+	if err != nil || modelID != settings.TtsModelID || current != identity {
+		cancel()
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "playback configuration changed")
+	}
+	if err = session.Append(c.Request().Context(), request.Frame); err != nil {
+		if errors.Is(err, context.Canceled) {
+			cancel()
+		}
+		return echo.NewHTTPError(http.StatusConflict, "playback input rejected")
+	}
+	return c.NoContent(http.StatusNoContent)
+}

+type lifecycleInputAudio struct {
+	*playbackAudio
+	url      string
+	opens    atomic.Int32
+	identity atomic.Value
+}
+
+func (a *lifecycleInputAudio) PlaybackIdentity(context.Context, string) (string, error) {
+	return a.identity.Load().(string), nil
+}
+
+func (a *lifecycleInputAudio) PlaybackInputInfo(context.Context, string) (string, string, error) {
+	return a.identity.Load().(string), "audio/mpeg", nil
+}
+
+func (a *lifecycleInputAudio) OpenPlaybackInput(ctx context.Context, _ string) (input.Session, string, error) {
+	a.opens.Add(1)
+	s, err := cosyvoiceinput.New("synthetic-only", a.url).OpenInput(ctx, sdk.SpeechParams{Model: &sdk.SpeechModel{ID: "configured-fixture-model"}, Config: map[string]any{"voice": "configured-fixture-voice"}})
+	return s, a.identity.Load().(string), err
+}

+func inputLifecycleServer(t *testing.T) (*httptest.Server, *lifecycleInputAudio, <-chan map[string]any, string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	commands := make(chan map[string]any, 16)
+	ws := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
+		c, err := (&websocket.Upgrader{}).Upgrade(w, r, nil)
+		if err != nil {
+			return
+		}
+		defer func() { _ = c.Close() }()
+		continues := 0
+		for {
+			var command map[string]any
+			if c.ReadJSON(&command) != nil {
+				return
+			}
+			commands <- command
+			header := command["header"].(map[string]any)
+			id := header["task_id"]
+			switch header["action"] {
+			case "run-task":
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-started", "task_id": id}})
+			case "continue-task":
+				continues++
+				if continues == 2 {
+					_ = c.WriteMessage(websocket.BinaryMessage, []byte{1, 2, 3, 4})
+				}
+			case "finish-task":
+				_ = c.WriteJSON(map[string]any{"header": map[string]any{"event": "task-finished", "task_id": id}})
+				return
+			}
+		}
+	}))
+	t.Cleanup(ws.Close)
+	audio := &lifecycleInputAudio{playbackAudio: &playbackAudio{}, url: "ws" + strings.TrimPrefix(ws.URL, "http")}
+	audio.identity.Store("fixture-config-one")
+	q := newInvocationTestQueries()
+	q.bot = testBotRow(invocationTestBotID, nil)
+	q.bot.OwnerUserID = testUUID(invocationTestOwnerID)
+	account := accounts.NewService(nil, &playbackBoundaryAccounts{active: true})
+	h := &BotAudioHandler{audioService: audio, settingsService: &playbackBoundarySettings{}}
+	h.SetPlaybackAccess(bots.NewService(nil, q), account)
+	const secret = "synthetic-native-input-loopback-boundary"
+	token, _, err := auth.GenerateToken(invocationTestOwnerID, secret, time.Minute)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	httpServer := httptest.NewServer(server.NewServerWithSessionValidator(slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler), "", secret, account.ValidateSession, h))
+	t.Cleanup(httpServer.Close)
+	return httpServer, audio, commands, token
+}
+
+func nativeRequest(t *testing.T, server *httptest.Server, token, method, id, text string, seq, offset uint64, final bool) *http.Response {
+	t.Helper()
+	body, err := json.Marshal(playbackInputRequest{ID: id, Frame: input.Frame{Sequence: seq, Offset: offset, Text: text, Final: final}})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(t.Context(), method, server.URL+"/bots/"+invocationTestBotID+"/tts/playback/input", strings.NewReader(string(body)))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
+	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	//nolint:gosec // G704: URL is the owned httptest loopback server, never request/user input.
+	response, err := (&http.Client{Timeout: 3 * time.Second}).Do(req)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	return response
+}
+
+func nextInputCommand(t *testing.T, commands <-chan map[string]any, action string) map[string]any {
+	t.Helper()
+	select {
+	case command := <-commands:
+		if command["header"].(map[string]any)["action"] != action {
+			t.Fatalf("unexpected command %v", command["header"])
+		}
+		return command
+	case <-time.After(time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("missing command " + action)
+		return nil
+	}
+}
+
+func TestNativeHTTPInputKeepsOneTaskAndStreamsBeforeFinishWithoutReopeningMissingOrDuplicateInput(t *testing.T) {
+	server, audio, commands, token := inputLifecycleServer(t)
+	const id = "77777777-7777-4777-8777-777777777777"
+	response := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPost, id, "甲😀", 0, 0, false)
+	defer func() { _ = response.Body.Close() }()
+	if response.StatusCode != http.StatusOK || response.Header.Get("X-Speech-Playback-Identity") != "fixture-config-one" {
+		t.Fatalf("response %v", response.StatusCode)
+	}
+	run := nextInputCommand(t, commands, "run-task")
+	nextInputCommand(t, commands, "continue-task")
+	// No audio has been sent yet: receiving headers proves the HTTP ingress is
+	// ready for the next frame without waiting on the model's first sound.
+	duplicate := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPost, id, "duplicate", 0, 0, false)
+	_ = duplicate.Body.Close()
+	if duplicate.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
+		t.Fatalf("duplicate %d", duplicate.StatusCode)
+	}
+	wrong := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPut, "88888888-8888-4888-8888-888888888888", "late", 1, 2, false)
+	_ = wrong.Body.Close()
+	if wrong.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
+		t.Fatalf("missing %d", wrong.StatusCode)
+	}
+	appendResponse := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPut, id, "乙。", 1, 2, false)
+	_ = appendResponse.Body.Close()
+	if appendResponse.StatusCode != http.StatusNoContent {
+		t.Fatalf("append %d", appendResponse.StatusCode)
+	}
+	next := nextInputCommand(t, commands, "continue-task")
+	if next["header"].(map[string]any)["task_id"] != run["header"].(map[string]any)["task_id"] {
+		t.Fatal("task restarted")
+	}
+	bytes := make([]byte, 4)
+	if _, err := io.ReadFull(response.Body, bytes); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if string(bytes) != string([]byte{1, 2, 3, 4}) {
+		t.Fatalf("audio %v", bytes)
+	}
+	final := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPut, id, "", 2, 4, true)
+	_ = final.Body.Close()
+	if final.StatusCode != http.StatusNoContent {
+		t.Fatalf("finish %d", final.StatusCode)
+	}
+	nextInputCommand(t, commands, "finish-task")
+	if _, err := io.ReadAll(response.Body); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	late := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPut, id, "late", 3, 4, false)
+	_ = late.Body.Close()
+	if late.StatusCode != http.StatusNotFound {
+		t.Fatalf("late %d", late.StatusCode)
+	}
+	if audio.opens.Load() != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("opens %d", audio.opens.Load())
+	}
+}
+
+func TestNativeHTTPConfigCancellationIsTruncatedEOFAndNeverACompleteCacheBody(t *testing.T) {
+	server, audio, commands, token := inputLifecycleServer(t)
+	const id = "99999999-9999-4999-8999-999999999999"
+	response := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPost, id, "甲😀", 0, 0, false)
+	defer func() { _ = response.Body.Close() }()
+	nextInputCommand(t, commands, "run-task")
+	nextInputCommand(t, commands, "continue-task")
+	audio.identity.Store("fixture-config-changed")
+	denied := nativeRequest(t, server, token, http.MethodPut, id, "乙。", 1, 2, false)
+	_ = denied.Body.Close()
+	if denied.StatusCode != http.StatusConflict {
+		t.Fatalf("configuration %d", denied.StatusCode)
+	}
+	if _, err := io.ReadAll(response.Body); err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("configuration cancellation became clean EOF and could be cached")
+	}
+	if audio.opens.Load() != 1 || len(commands) != 0 {
+		t.Fatal("cancellation opened or appended another synthesis")
+	}
+}
